Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the cohomologies structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 2. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 87 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).
